### [150] [Token Budget Saturation and Mechanistic Early Detection of Reasoning Non-Convergence in Chain-of-Thought Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21433)
*Renuka Oladri,Niveda Jawahar,Abdirisak Mohamed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B等思维链推理模型存在双峰收敛模式：收敛的生成在AIME数据集上准确率90.3%，非收敛的仅6.6%。通过探测模型内部表示，发现token 150处第20层激活的AUC为0.608，表明收敛结果在生成早期即可部分预测，为早期退出推理和自适应计算分配提供了可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究思维链推理模型中存在的双峰收敛现象（生成要么在预算内终止，要么耗尽预算未得出结论），并试图在生成早期通过模型内部表征检测收敛结果，以实现自适应计算分配。

Method: 在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B模型上，训练线性探针（linear probes）于隐藏状态激活（token位置50-300），特别是第20层在token 150处的激活，并使用AUC和置换检验评估预测性能，同时与基于token熵和重复统计的行为基线进行比较。

Result: 收敛生成在AIME 1983-2024上准确率90.3%，非收敛仅6.6%，总体收敛率62.0%。线性探针在token 150处第20层激活达到AUC 0.608（±0.080，5折交叉验证），即使在token 50也有可靠表现。激活探针一致优于行为基线。全扫描置换检验p=0.063（100,000次置换）。

Conclusion: 收敛命运在生成结束前已部分编码于中间表征中，这为早期退出推理和自适应计算分配开辟了可行路径，但信号较弱，样本量不足以在常规阈值下确认。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning models such as DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B exhibit a bimodal convergence pattern: generations either terminate within a token budget (converged) or exhaust it without reaching a conclusion (non-converged). We characterize this phenomenon empirically, showing that converged generations achieve 90.3% accuracy on AIME 1983-2024 while non-converged ones achieve only 6.6%, with an overall convergence rate of 62.0%. We then ask whether this outcome is detectable early in the thinking chain using internal model representations. Training linear probes on hidden-state activations at token positions 50-300, we find that layer-20 activations at token 150 achieve AUC 0.608 (+-0.080, 5-fold CV), reliably above chance even at token 50. Activation probes consistently outperform behavioral baselines derived from token entropy and repetition statistics. A sweep-level permutation test yields p=0.063 (100,000 permutations), consistent with a modest signal that our sample size cannot confirm at conventional thresholds. These findings suggest that convergence fate is partially encoded in intermediate representations well before the generation ends, opening a path toward early-exit inference and adaptive compute allocation.

</details>


### [151] [When Trivia Is Not Trivial: Everyday Knowledge Failures in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21445)
*Anna Mosolova,Djamé Seddah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个多语言常识与长尾知识问答基准TriviaRoomQA，评估了30个开源大语言模型在288个主题上的表现，发现模型在历史、地理等知识密集型主题上表现强劲，但在流行文化、娱乐等方面显著薄弱，且知识获取存在语言依赖性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学术基准已趋于饱和，缺乏对常识性、文化性和长尾知识的系统评估，因此需要构建一个能够反映真实世界知识广度与语言多样性的评测基准。

Method: 构建了TriviaRoomQA基准，包含3,300道多语言并行选择题（覆盖6种欧洲语言）和5,340道法语专属题，涵盖288个主题；评估了30个来自欧亚北美供应商的开源大语言模型（7B-70B参数）。

Result: 模型在历史、地理、数学等知识密集型主题上表现强劲，但在名人、音乐、电影、新闻等流行文化主题上明显薄弱；且相同问题的表现随语言不同而变化，知识获取并非完全语言无关。

Conclusion: 现有模型存在重要的知识缺口（尤其在流行文化与跨语言一致性方面），这是当前学术导向的饱和基准所无法捕捉的。

Abstract: Quiz rooms, trivia nights, and quiz shows challenge human knowledge across a wide range of topics, from canonical facts to everyday culture. In this paper, we examine whether large language models (LLMs) can perform competitively in such settings, using quiz-style questions to test them on both common and niche topics. We introduce TriviaRoomQA, a multilingual benchmark designed to evaluate everyday, culturally grounded, and long-tail knowledge across 288 topics. The benchmark contains 3,300 parallel multiple-choice questions in six European languages and additional 5,340 French-only questions for a more fine-grained case study. We evaluate 30 open-weight LLMs from European, Asian, and North American providers, covering models from 7 to 70B parameters. We find that models are strong on knowledge-intensive topics such as history, geography, and mathematics, but substantially weaker on everyday popular-culture topics such as celebrities, music, movies, and news. Moreover, model performance varies across languages even for the same underlying questions, suggesting that access to factual knowledge is not always language-independent. In sum, our dataset and experiments demonstrate an important knowledge gap which is not captured by existing academic-based saturated benchmarks.

</details>


### [152] [RUMBA: Russian User Memory Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21447)
*Elizaveta Shevtsova,Inna Glebkina,Mark Baushenko,Pavel Gulyaev,Alena Fenogenova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RUMBA是一个面向长时对话记忆的俄语基准测试，包含细粒度分类体系和统一评估方法，用于诊断模型在跨会话检索、组合与推理方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM长时记忆基准以英语为中心，依赖聚合检索指标，未能捕捉长程上下文、时间信息与推理之间的交互作用。

Method: 构建了带有时间戳的用户-助手对话数据集，设计了细粒度的记忆问题分类法，并提出了统一评估方法，涵盖语义类型、会话范围、时间推理和时间表达显式性四个维度。

Result: 对当代记忆系统和长上下文模型进行了评估，验证了RUMBA作为诊断工具的有效性，能够识别不同记忆机制的优势和失败模式。

Conclusion: RUMBA为俄语长时对话记忆评估提供了高质量基准，其对齐的英语子集支持跨语言分析，能够深入诊断模型在不同记忆维度上的行为。

Abstract: The ability to handle long-term memory in LLMs is becoming increasingly critical, yet existing benchmarks remain English-centric and rely on aggregate retrieval metrics, failing to capture interactions between long-range context, temporal information, and reasoning. To address this, we introduce RUMBA (Russian User Memory BenchmArk) - a new benchmark for long-term conversational memory that provides a fine-grained taxonomy of memory-centric question types and a unified methodology accounting for semantic type, session scope, temporal reasoning, and the explicitness of temporal expressions. RUMBA consists of timestamped user-assistant dialogues with QA pairs requiring retrieval, combination, and reasoning across sessions. While designed for Russian, we also provide an aligned English subset under the same methodology. We evaluate contemporary memory systems and long-context models, and show how RUMBA serves as a diagnostic tool to analyze model behavior across benchmark slices and identify strengths and failure modes of different memory mechanisms.

</details>


### [153] [What, Where, and How: Disentangling the Roles of Task, Language, and Model in Code Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21491)
*Piotr Wilam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过在2x2设计（Python/Rust × Qwen2.5-Coder-7B/DeepSeek-Coder-V1-6.7B）中对比代码语言模型的神经回路，发现任务决定哪些概念获得专用回路（“什么”是通用的），而模型决定回路位置和增长方式（“在哪”和“如何”不通用）。还发现Rust比Python有更多概念专用回路，两模型间存在神经元共享等新现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究独立训练的语言模型是否以相同方式表征相同事物，特别是从任务、语言、模型三个维度分离影响因素。

Method: 采用概念电路提取方法，构建2x2设计（两种编程语言×两个模型），对58个Python和57个Rust语法概念进行完整电路测量，结合消融实验和线性探针验证功能性。

Result: 1) 任务决定哪些概念有专用电路（Spearman ρ≈0.65）；2) 模型决定电路位置（Qwen在L17-19，DeepSeek在L6-7）和增长模式；3) Rust比Python多2-3倍概念专用电路；4) 两模型共享语言间神经元；5) Qwen将Rust的9个类型/特质关键词绑定为一个紧密神经元簇。

Conclusion: 电路通用性不是简单的是/否问题：表征内容（What）是通用的，但计算组织（Where和How）取决于具体模型。通用性是表征内容的属性，而非计算组织的属性。

Abstract: Do independently trained language models come to represent the same thing in the same way? We answer for code, extending a recently introduced concept-circuit extraction method to a 2x2 design -- Python and Rust crossed with Qwen2.5-Coder-7B and DeepSeek-Coder-V1-6.7B -- and measuring a complete inventory of grammatical concepts (58 Python, 57 Rust) identically in all four cells: the smallest design that separates what depends on the task, the language, and the model.
  The answer splits into three parts. What earns dedicated circuitry is set by the task: the models agree on which concepts receive circuits (Spearman $ρ$ = 0.638 for Python, 0.673 for Rust, both p < $10^{-7}$). Where those circuits sit is set by the model: Qwen processes concepts in a late band (~L17-19), DeepSeek at L6-7, for both languages. How circuits grow across layers is also set by the model: Qwen gives its atomic concepts an early spike that DeepSeek does not. "Are circuits universal?" thus has no single answer: yes for What, no for Where and How -- universality is a property of representational content, not of computational organisation.
  None of this structure was fixed in advance. The agreement could have landed anywhere between independence and identity; it lands at $ρ\approx 0.65$. Rust constructs receive 2-3x more concept-specific circuitry than their Python equivalents, in both models. Both models share neurons between the languages (6/7 and 7/7 paired constructs), DeepSeek 1.94x more than Qwen -- a direction no prior result predicts. And Qwen binds nine keywords of Rust's type-and-trait machinery into one tight neuron cluster (Jaccard 0.535 vs null 0.112, p < 0.001), a semantic dimension invisible in surface syntax. Ablation and linear probes confirm the circuits are functional.
  All claims are scoped to this 2x2; whether the per-model profile predicts a third model is the designed next test.

</details>


### [154] [Artificial Epanorthosis: Why large language models overuse a classical rhetorical figure, and how to mitigate it](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21498)
*Federico Boggia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型过度使用一种古典修辞手法——epanorthosis（自我修正），分析了其成因（训练数据偏好、RLHF强化、自回归生成机制），提出了Epanorthosis Index评分方法，测量发现模型在不同体裁中与人类基线存在校准偏差，并展示了通过LoRA微调可将该修辞频率恢复至人类水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成文本中系统性地过度使用一种两千年前的修辞手法（epanorthosis），作者旨在探究其成因并解决模型与人类修辞风格之间的偏差问题。

Method: 通过Epanorthosis Index（修辞密度与人类基线的比值）进行跨体裁测量，比较不同模型规模的校准表现；使用轻量LoRA适配器进行微调，配合单行指令和SFT适配器进行修辞频率调控，并引入缩放系数实现精确校准。

Result: 模型在演讲类文本中过度使用（英文约2倍，意大利文近3倍，且集中在较大模型上），在非正式问答中则使用不足；在议论文、新闻和百科类文本中与人类持平。单行指令可将修辞频率降低一半至近四分之三，SFT适配器几乎完全消除，缩放系数可将其精确回调至人类水平。

Conclusion: 目标不是消除模型中的修辞手法，而是使其与每种体裁的人类基线校准；真正的风险是人类开始模仿机器的写作风格。

Abstract: A rhetorical figure that Cicero and Quintilian catalogued two thousand years ago reappears, systematically, in the text of large language models: epanorthosis, the self-correction of the specimen «This is not a course. It is a journey of transformation». This essay argues that the overuse is a trained disposition, driven mainly by a training distribution rich in promotional prose and by preference tuning (RLHF) that rewards confident, emphatic phrasing; the left-to-right nature of generation is an amplifier rather than the root cause. Building on evidence that models diverge from human rhetorical style, and on Fontanier's classification of epanorthosis as a figure of thought, it sets out a programme that scores the figure against genre-specific human baselines through an Epanorthosis Index (density relative to the human rate). A first measurement, on three sizes of one instruction-tuned model family, finds mis-calibration by register in both directions: the models overshoot in oratory (about twofold, near threefold in Italian, concentrated in the larger tiers) and undershoot in informal question-and-answer writing, while matching humans in argument, journalism, and encyclopedic prose. Three constructive contributions follow: a survey of mitigation techniques centred on lightweight LoRA adapters; a demonstration, in Italian, that a one-line instruction cuts the figure by half to nearly three-quarters and that a supervised-fine-tuning adapter removes it almost entirely, with a scaling coefficient that dials the reduction back onto the human rate; and the argument that the target is calibration to the human rate for each genre, not elimination. It closes on the stakes: the real risk is that we begin to write like the machines.

</details>


### [155] [DONDO: Open w2v-BERT Speech-Recognition Base Models for African Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21540)
*Paul Azunre*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DONDO是一个基于w2v-BERT 2.0的开放ASR模型家族，包含21个单语和5个多语模型，覆盖非洲27种语言变体，通过两阶段微调策略和语言条件机制实现多语种语音识别，在Apache-2.0许可下发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲语言缺乏转录语音数据，现有ASR模型覆盖不足，需要开发开放、许可宽松的ASR基础模型以支持这些语言。

Method: 基于w2v-BERT 2.0自监督语音编码器，使用宗教文本中的朗读语音进行微调；采用两阶段（部分三阶段）学习率退火微调策略；引入轻量级语言条件机制，通过将one-hot语言身份作为前缀帧注入声学特征来引导多语言模型。

Result: 五个多语言家族的退火模型平均词错误率（WER）达到10-13%，基本缩小了与单语模型的差距，且单个检查点可覆盖多种语言。

Conclusion: DONDO成功为非洲27种语言变体提供了开放ASR模型，覆盖约一亿第一语言使用者，为非洲语言语音识别领域做出了重要贡献。

Abstract: We present DONDO, a family of open, permissively licensed automatic speech recognition (ASR) base models for African languages, built on the w2v-BERT 2.0 self-supervised speech encoder. DONDO comprises twenty-one monolingual models and five multilingual models spanning twenty-seven language varieties across Ghana, Sierra Leone, Nigeria, Senegal, Kenya and Zimbabwe. Models are fine-tuned primarily on read speech drawn from religious texts, which offer broad, license-clear and orthographically consistent coverage for languages that otherwise lack transcribed audio. We describe a two-step (and, for one family, three-step) learning-rate-annealed fine-tuning procedure that first adapts a shared multilingual model at a high learning rate and then anneals it to recover, and in several cases surpass, strong monolingual baselines. We further describe a lightweight language-conditioning mechanism that injects a one-hot language identity as a sequence of prefix frames prepended to the acoustic features, allowing a single multilingual checkpoint to be steered to a target language at inference. Across the five multilingual families the annealed models reach average word error rates (WER) of 10-13%, closing most of the gap to monolingual models while covering many languages in a single checkpoint. All models are released on the Hugging Face KhayaAI organisation under the Apache-2.0 license (attribution only) so that others may fine-tune them freely, including for commercial use. We provide a conservative estimate that the languages covered are spoken by on the order of one hundred million first-language speakers, and by substantially more when second-language use is included.

</details>


### [156] [MedGame: Storytelling Gamification Empowered by Large Language Models for Medical Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21570)
*Qian Wu,Xinrong Zhou,Zizhan Ma,Kai Chen,Zheyao Gao,Xun Lin,Hongqiu Wu,Longfei Gou,Yixiao Liu,Ann Sin Nga Lau,Qi Dou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MedGame是一个将临床病例转化为结构化、可执行的叙事游戏框架，采用双引擎设计（医学叙事设计器+故事导演），构建了5000病例基准测试集，实验表明微调可提升开源模型性能，学生认为比纯文本更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM医学教育系统多局限于问答或单轮反馈，缺乏将完整临床病例组织成以决策为中心的学习轨迹的能力。

Method: 提出MedGame框架，包含Medical Narrative Designer（生成病例故事线及决策节点）和Story Director（转化为依赖感知的多模态编排计划），并构建包含5000病例的MedGame Bench评估基准。

Result: 任务特定微调显著提升了开源LLM在MedGame Bench上的表现，缩小了与商业模型的差距；学生实验显示MedGame比纯文本方案更具参与感和实用性。

Conclusion: 叙事游戏化方法能有效提升医学教育中LLM的应用效果，通过结构化的临床故事线增强学习者的决策训练体验。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for medical education, but most existing systems focus on localized interactions such as question answering or single-turn feedback, rather than organizing an entire clinical case into a decision-centered learning trajectory. We introduce \textit{MedGame}, a framework that transforms static clinical cases into structured, executable storytelling games. MedGame uses a dual-engine design: a Medical Narrative Designer synthesizes case-grounded clinical storylines with states and decision nodes, while a Story Director converts them into dependency-aware multimodal orchestration plans rendered by our released interactive platform. We construct MedGame Bench, a 5,000-case benchmark and evaluation protocol for Medical Narrative Generation and Story Direction. Experiments show that task-specific fine-tuning substantially improves open-source LLMs on MedGame Bench and narrows the gap with commercial models. A pilot student study further shows that learners perceive MedGame as more engaging and useful than text-only alternatives.

</details>


### [157] [Surprisal Theory is Tautological (without Rational Grounding)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21574)
*Ryan Cotterell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文论证了惊奇理论（Surprisal Theory）本质上是一种同义反复（tautology），因为对于任何非负的难度度量，总存在一个语言模型使其惊奇值与难度呈仿射关系，导致该理论无法做出可证伪的预测。打破这种循环需要理性主义干预，即语言模型必须来自非经验驱动的理解者模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示惊奇理论在无额外约束下是同义反复，无法做出可证伪的预测，并指出过去二十年研究中隐含的假设（训练语料分布即相关语言模型）已被近期实证推翻。

Method: 通过形式论证表明：对于任意非负难度度量，在温和技术条件下总存在一个语言模型使其惊奇值为该度量的仿射函数；再结合近期实证（更好的语料模型反而预测更差）来瓦解隐含假设。

Result: 惊奇理论若无额外约束是不可证伪的；更好拟合语料分布的模型反而可能更差地预测人类处理难度。

Conclusion: 要打破同义反复，必须引入理性主义干预，即语言模型应源自非经验驱动的理解者理论（如记忆约束或处理目标），而非依赖于要解释的行为数据。

Abstract: Surprisal theory holds that the human processing difficulty of a linguistic unit in context is an affine function of its surprisal under some language model. I argue this claim is a tautology without further constraint: for any non-negative difficulty measure over units in context, there exists a language model whose surprisal is an affine function of it under mild technical conditions. Therefore, because any pattern of difficulty is consistent with some language model, without an additional constraint on the language model, surprisal theory makes no falsifiable predictions. The tautology was long obscured by an assumption implicit in two decades of psycholinguistic work---that the relevant language model is the distribution that generated the training corpus, so that improving corpus fit improves predictions of human behavior. Recent empirical work has undermined this assumption, demonstrating that better corpus models can be worse predictors of processing difficulty. I conclude that breaking the tautology requires a rationalist intervention, i.e., the relevant language model must be derived from a non-empirically motivated model of the comprehender, which could be based on, for instance, memory constraints or processing goals, and that, thus, does not depend on the behavioral data surprisal theory is meant to explain.

</details>


### [158] [Knowledge Injection Exists in MoE? Exploring Expert-Aware Contrast Decoding in MoE for Mitigating LLMs'Hallucinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20426)
*Xinyue Fang,Zhiliang Tian,Zhen Huang,Ziyi Pan,Zhihua Wen,Xi Wang,Quntian Fang,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EAACD方法，利用MoE模型高层专家的激活模式差异进行对比解码，有效缓解LLM幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉缓解方法要么难以改变模型内部知识，要么跨领域泛化能力差；对比解码虽有效但此前仅探索了Transformer模型，未研究MoE模型。

Method: 提出EAACD（专家感知自适应对比解码），将MoE高层专家按置信度和一致性分为高可靠组和多个低可靠组，通过高可靠组与各低可靠组的预测对比来校准原始预测，并利用注意力机制和掩码放大低可靠专家的幻觉以增强对比效果。

Result: 在四个数据集上全面优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: MoE模型中共享专家不存在层间差异，但不同MoE的高层在事实性与非事实性输出上表现出不同的专家激活模式，EAACD利用这一发现有效缓解了幻觉。

Abstract: Existing LLM hallucination mitigation methods, including prompt engineering and model optimization, either hardly alter models'internal knowledge or have poor cross-domain generalization. Contrastive decoding mitigates hallucinations by using layer-wise differences in LLMs. However, prior studies only explore transformer-based models (e.g., GPT), ignoring other effective frameworks like mixture-of-experts (MoE) models. Since MoE alters the traditional transformer architecture, we conduct empirical studies to investigate whether similar layer-wise differences exist in MoEs. Our results show that they do not exist in MoE with shared experts; nevertheless, across different MoEs, higher layers exhibit distinct expert activation patterns between factual and non-factual outputs. Building on these, we propose EAACD, an expert-aware adaptive contrast decoding that uses expert differences in MoE's higher layers to mitigate hallucinations on QA tasks. EAACD splits high-layer experts into a higher-reliability group and several lower-reliability groups based on their confidence and consistency. It contrasts the higher-reliability group's prediction with each lower-reliability group's prediction to calibrate the model's original predictions. To strengthen this contrast, EAACD amplifies hallucinations from lower-reliability experts via attention and masking to provide stronger negative references. EAACD outperforms all baselines on four datasets.

</details>


### [159] [Is MoE Routing a Huffman Code? Discovering the Frequency-Diversity Law in Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20427)
*Ching-Chieh Tsao,Zhuoyi Lin,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现MoE路由的本质是霍夫曼编码的体现，并提出频率-多样性定律：模型为常见token分配稀疏专家，为稀有复杂任务分配高多样性专家。针对负载均衡导致的冗余问题，提出子集差异剪枝策略，消除功能重复，释放潜在霍夫曼效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构的路由机制目前仍是黑箱，缺乏对其底层逻辑的理论理解。作者希望揭示MoE路由的内在运行原理。

Method: 提出频率-多样性定律解释路由行为；针对Qwen3.5-35B-A3B的冗余陷阱，提出子集差异剪枝策略消除功能重复专家。

Result: 剪枝不仅不损害推理能力，反而迫使逻辑坍缩为精简、高密度的路径，释放模型潜在的霍夫曼效率。

Conclusion: 下一代MoE应从强制负载均衡转向最小描述长度最优性，将路由从启发式转变为有原则的压缩引擎。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts architectures have revolutionized scaling, yet the underlying logic of their routing remains a black box. In this paper, we uncover a fundamental governing principle: MoE routing is not merely selection, but a manifestation of Huffman Coding. We introduce the Frequency-Diversity Law, revealing that state-of-the-art models, such as Phi-3.5-MoE and Gemma-4-27B-A4B, spontaneously act as information-theoretic engines. These models allocate sparse expert resources for common tokens while invoking high-diversity expert committees for rare, complex tasks found in chain-of-thought trajectories. However, we identify a critical redundancy trap in Qwen3.5-35B-A3B: when effective sparsity (k/E_eff) is sufficiently low, load-balancing inadvertently imposes functional redundancy, masking the underlying Huffman efficiency signal. To bridge this gap, we propose Subset Difference Pruning, a surgical strategy to eliminate functional duplicates. We demonstrate that pruning does not degrade reasoning; instead, it unleashes the model's latent Huffman efficiency, forcing the logic to collapse into streamlined, high-density paths. Our findings suggest that the next generation of MoEs should move beyond forced load-balancing toward Minimum Description Length (MDL) optimality, assigning shorter expert-routing codes to high-frequency information and longer, more diverse codes to low-frequency information, thereby transforming routing from a heuristic into a principled compression engine.

</details>


### [160] [More Is Not More: What Matters for Diversity in LLM Opinions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20429)
*Qiyang Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过析因实验，系统研究了提升LLM输出多样性的两种干预维度（输入条件与交互架构），发现：人物角色细节并非越多越好，不同交互架构探索的见解区域不重叠，常见的低成本方法（如提高采样温度）效果甚微。多样性需通过结构化的组合干预实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM输出存在系统性意见同质化问题，虽然已有多种多样性提升方法，但缺乏统一评估框架，不同方法被孤立评估且使用不可比的指标，实际中多种干预同时部署时难以归因效果。

Method: 设计析因实验，将干预分为两个维度：输入条件（通过角色深度操作化）和交互架构。在100个真实用户开放性问题上评估7个模型，使用多种互补指标衡量多样性。

Result: 1) 人物角色细节并非越多越好，初始角色条件已捕获大部分增益，进一步增加人口统计细节会降低某些模型的多样性。2) 不同交互架构探索的见解区域不重叠，组合多种架构比优化单一架构覆盖更广。3) 提高采样温度、添加多样性指令等低成本方法效果远低于结构化干预。

Conclusion: 多样性不是沿单一维度缩放的结果，而是高度依赖于干预的结构形式和组合方式。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to simulate diverse human opinions in open-ended tasks such as synthetic surveys, focus group modeling, and public opinion prediction. However, LLM outputs exhibit systematic opinion homogenization. Practitioners have explored various interventions to increase diversity, but the landscape remains fragmented: different methods are evaluated in isolation with incomparable metrics, and in practice they are typically deployed and upgraded simultaneously, making it difficult to attribute gains to specific components. To advance a more scientific understanding of LLM output diversity, we design a factorial experiment that separates two primary intervention dimensions: input conditioning (operationalized through persona depth) and interaction architecture. We evaluate all conditions on 100 real-user open-ended questions across 7 models, measuring diversity with multiple complementary metrics. Our findings challenge several common assumptions. First, more persona detail does not monotonically increase diversity. The initial step of persona conditioning already captures the majority of the gain, while further elaboration with demographic detail does not consistently improve and can reduce diversity on some models. Second, rather than seeking a single best interaction architecture, we find that different architectures explore largely non-overlapping opinion regions. Combining multiple architectures yields broader coverage than optimizing any one. Third, commonly attempted low-cost alternatives such as raising sampling temperature and adding diversity instructions produce negligible effects compared to structured interventions. Overall, our work demonstrates that diversity is not a product of scaling along any single dimension, but is highly sensitive to the structural form and combination of interventions.

</details>


### [161] [LLM-INSTRUCT at UZH Shared Task 2026: Constraint-Aware Retrieval and Selective Debate for Paragraph-Level Argument Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20430)
*Phuong Huu Vu Tran,Long Minh Vo,Son Nguyen Minh Le,Hoang Van*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-INSTRUCT系统，在ArgMining 2026的UZH共享任务中，通过元数据检索缩小候选标签空间、约束解码、三智能体辩论分支和模式验证，在段落级论辩挖掘任务中获得综合第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对联合国和联合国教科文组织决议中段落级论辩挖掘任务，需要在严格JSON格式约束下使用不超过8B参数的开源模型，完成段落类型分类、141个官方标签的子集预测和有向关系预测。

Method: 将任务建模为约束结构预测：1) 元数据感知的稠密检索缩小候选标签空间；2) 每维度上限的约束解码；3) 仅不确定案例升级至三智能体辩论分支；4) 最终输出模式验证。

Result: 官方排行榜综合第一，F1第一，LLM-as-a-Judge第五；开发中Task 1b Micro-F1从35.83%提升至40.08%，Task 2内部得分保持4.421。

Conclusion: 核心经验：在生成前减少决策空间能同时提升准确率和提交鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present LLM-INSTRUCT, the winning system for the UZH Shared Task at ArgMining 2026 on paragraph-level argument mining in UN and UNESCO resolutions. The task requires paragraph-type classification, prediction of a subset of 141 official tags, and directed relation prediction under a strict JSON schema setting using only open-weight models up to 8B parameters. We frame the task as constrained structured prediction. The system first narrows the candidate tag space with metadata-aware dense retrieval, then applies constrained decoding with per-dimension caps, escalates only uncertain cases to a three-agent debate branch, and finally validates the output schema. On the official leaderboard, LLM-INSTRUCT ranked 1st overall, with 1st in F1 and 5th in LLM-as-a-Judge. During development, our configuration search further improved Task 1b Micro-F1 from 35.83% to 40.08% while keeping the internal Task 2 score at 4.421. The main lesson is simple: reducing the decision space before generation improves both accuracy and submission robustness. Our code and supporting scripts are publicly available at: https://github.com/LLM-Instruct-at-UZH-Shared-Task-2026/Method

</details>


### [162] [Moir: Let the Model Direct Its Own Story for Robust Cross-Domain Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20433)
*Jea Kwon,Jiwon Kim,Dong-kyum Kim,Meeyoung Cha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Moir方法，通过从模型自身的解码分布中采样来估计协方差矩阵，从而解决知识编辑中模型非对称性能退化（数学和编程推理能力下降）的问题。在多个模型和编辑方法上，Moir显著提升了编辑后的推理保留能力，尤其是Qwen3-8B在20,000次编辑后GSM8K准确率从10.9%提升至79.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识编辑会导致模型核心能力（数学推理和编程推理）严重退化，而百科类事实记忆保持完好。作者将这种非对称退化归因于协方差编辑器的分布不匹配：现有编辑方法使用的协方差仅覆盖参考语料（如Wikipedia）的子空间，无法捕捉模型经过SFT/DPO后对齐的"操作分布"（operative distribution）。

Method: 提出Moir，通过从模型自身的解码分布中采样来估计保留协方差矩阵C。具体做法是：用单个随机词汇token作为种子生成文本，绕过指令跟随模板对输出的主导影响，从而暴露模型内化的更广泛子空间。Moir无需任何外部数据，可作为任何协方差编辑器的即插即用组件。

Result: 在OLMo-2、Llama-3.1和Qwen-3（7-8B）上，结合MEMIT和AlphaEdit，在批量和顺序编辑模式下，Moir均能持续提升最脆弱领域的保留能力。最突出结果：Qwen3-8B在20,000次AlphaEdit批量编辑后，GSM8K准确率从Wikipedia基线的10.9%提升至79.9%。

Conclusion: 对齐保留分布与模型的操作分布是无损编辑的关键因素，而模型本身可能是部署系统中获取该分布的最可行来源。

Abstract: While language models remain frozen at their training state, the world evolves continuously. Knowledge editing has emerged as a key alternative to full retraining, but its deployment is bottlenecked by the erosion of core capabilities: mathematical and programmatic reasoning collapse while encyclopedic recall remains intact. We trace this asymmetric degradation to a distributional mismatch. Covariance-based editors preserve only the subspaces spanned by their reference corpus, but fail to capture the operative distribution shaped by post-training such as SFT and DPO. Static external corpora, including Wikipedia and even the original pretraining mixture, cannot recover this shifted manifold. We propose Moir, which estimates the preservation covariance $C$ directly from the model itself by sampling from its own decoding distribution. Seeding generation with a single random vocabulary token bypasses the instruction-following templates that otherwise dominate sampled outputs, exposing the broader subspaces the model has internalized. Moir requires no external data and serves as a drop-in component for any covariance-based editor, a practical advantage given that the pre- and post-training corpora of most modern LLMs are not publicly accessible. Across OLMo-2, Llama-3.1, and Qwen-3 (7-8B), under both MEMIT and AlphaEdit and in batch and sequential regimes, Moir consistently extends preservation in the most vulnerable domains, most strikingly on Qwen3-8B after 20,000 AlphaEdit batch edits, it retains 79.9% GSM8K accuracy compared to 10.9% with the Wikipedia baseline. These results suggest that aligning the preservation distribution with the model's operative distribution is a key factor in non-destructive editing, and that the model itself may be the most accessible source of that distribution for deployed systems.

</details>


### [163] [Break Through the Compression Bottleneck: From Theory to Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20434)
*Xiusheng Huang,Lu Wang,Yequan Wang,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次从数学上证明低秩分解和量化两种模型压缩方法并非正交，它们的组合会导致性能显著下降，并提出了对角粘附方法（DAM）来缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型参数规模持续增长，需要有效的模型压缩方法来降低计算和内存开销。现有压缩方法存在瓶颈：当压缩比提高时，性能会显著下降。低秩分解和量化是两种有效的压缩方法，但它们的组合效果尚不明确，开发人员普遍假设它们是正交的。

Method: 首次从数学上证明了低秩分解与量化之间的非正交性，并通过一系列大型语言模型实验验证了这一发现。提出了对角线粘附方法（DAM），能够有效结合两种方法并减轻性能损失。

Result: 实验证明低秩分解和量化是非正交的，它们的组合会导致显著的性能下降。提出的DAM方法能够有效缓解这种性能损失。

Conclusion: 该研究为模型压缩提供了深刻见解，为未来相关研究奠定了坚实的理论和实验基础。

Abstract: As the parameter size of language models continues to grow, effective model compression is required to reduce their computational and memory overhead. Existing compression methods suffer from bottleneck issues: when the compression ratio is increased, performance degrades significantly. Low-rank decomposition and quantization are two prominent compression methods that have been proven to significantly reduce the computational and memory requirements of Large Language Models (LLMs) while maintaining model accuracy. Evidently, combining these two methods will break through the existing compression bottleneck. However, how these two methods interact when combined remains a critical question for developers, as many assume they are orthogonal, meaning their combination would not introduce additional errors beyond those independently introduced by each method. This paper provides the first mathematical proof that low-rank decomposition and quantization are non-orthogonal. We validate these findings through a series of experiments on large language models. Our results demonstrate that these methods are non-orthogonal, and their combination leads to significant performance degradation. Importantly, we propose a novel approach Diagonal Adhesive Method (DAM), which can effectively combine the two methods and mitigate the performance loss. Our research provides deep insights into model compression and lays a solid theoretical and experimental foundation for future related studies.

</details>


### [164] [Preference Tuning as Spectral Update Reorganization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20438)
*Peiyan Zhang,Haibo Jin,Liying Kang,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过谱分解方法研究RLHF等偏好优化训练中的参数更新结构，发现更新矩阵呈现头-尾（head-tail）谱组织：主导的头部分承担主要行为偏移，残差尾部分则贡献较弱但不可缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的偏好优化训练关注最终行为表现，但对学习到的参数更新本身的结构缺乏理解，作者希望打开这个"黑箱"。

Method: 对有效的LoRA更新进行谱分解，将谱成分作为可插拔模块进行隔离、重组和直接干预，跨模型、优化算法和监督方式进行分析。

Result: 1) 更新矩阵一致地呈现头-尾谱组织；2) 头部主导端点行为偏移，尾部单独作用弱；3) 跨运行重组时，混合适配器遵循头的来源；4) 仅头部学习非平凡但无法恢复完整解，尤其OOD表现差；5) 仅尾部学习几乎无可见提升，但缺少尾部也无法恢复完整解。

Conclusion: 偏好后训练应被理解为结构化的更新重组而非单一行为矫正，对齐增益与覆盖损失与学习更新的组织结构紧密相关。

Abstract: Preference-based post-training is usually understood through endpoint behavior, yet the learned update that produces this behavior remains largely opaque. We study RLHF and related preference optimization through the spectral structure of their induced parameter updates. By decomposing effective LoRA updates and reloading their spectral components as plug-in modules, we turn preference-induced updates into objects that can be isolated, recomposed, and directly intervened on. Across model families, optimization algorithms, and supervision regimes, these updates consistently develop a spectral head--tail organization. A compact head emerges early and carries the dominant endpoint shift, while a heterogeneous residual tail remains. The split is functional rather than merely descriptive. Plug-in intervention shows that the head accounts for the visible behavioral departure from the base model, while the tail is weak in isolation. Cross-run recomposition further shows that mixed adapters follow the source of the head, indicating that the head carries run-level solver bias. This endpoint dominance does not imply learning sufficiency. Head-only learning is non-vacuous but fails to recover the full solution, especially on out-of-distribution behavior. Tail-only learning yields little visible gain, yet the full solution is not recovered without the tail. These findings recast preference post-training as structured update reorganization rather than a monolithic behavioral correction, and suggest that alignment gain and coverage loss are tied to how the learned update itself is organized.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [JAXBench: Benchmarking Autonomous TPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20466)
*Arya Tschand,Charles Hong,Julian Walker,Nina Cai,Shangkun Wang,Suvinay Subramanian,Sundar Dev,Vijay Janapa Reddi,Amir Yazdanbakhsh,Sethu Sankaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了JAXBench——一个专门针对Google Cloud TPU的AI生成内核优化基准测试套件，包含50个JAX工作负载。研究发现，对于Pallas这种文档稀疏的DSL，目标特定的上下文比模型规模更重要：通过条件化TPU文档，正确率从5.8%提升到37.3%，解决48/50个基准问题，几何平均加速1.28倍。结合搜索方法的Autocomp达到1.36倍几何平均加速，在8个人工调优内核上恢复至专家上限的绝大部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核性能优化领域已有成熟的基准测试（如KernelBench），但TPU（张量处理单元）缺乏类似的标准化基准套件，限制了AI生成内核优化方法在TPU上的研究和进展。

Method: 从MaxText库中提取17个生产级ML算子（来自Llama-3.1、DeepSeek-V3等架构），并从KernelBench翻译33个算子并设定适配TPU v6e的新问题规模。使用Pallas DSL编写内核，评估了四种基于反馈的内核生成方法，并对比了不同上下文策略和搜索结构的效果。

Result: 条件化TPU文档将正确率从5.8%提升至37.3%，解决48/50个基准。Autocomp束搜索达到1.36x几何平均加速（相对于XLA），在8个手调内核上达到1.60x，接近2.08x的专家上限。

Conclusion: 高质量TPU内核优化仍是挑战性任务，对于文档稀疏的DSL，目标相关上下文比模型规模更关键，搜索结构在正确性基础上能带来显著收益。开源JAXBench及评估工具以促进社区贡献。

Abstract: Rigorous benchmarks have driven progress in autonomous GPU kernel performance optimization by establishing a shared target to hillclimb on, but no equivalent exists for TPUs. We present JAXBench, a TPU-native benchmark suite for AI-generated kernel optimization on Google Cloud TPUs. JAXBench comprises 50 JAX workloads that are both relevant and provide headroom for optimization. We extract 17 production ML operators from architectures in the public MaxText library such as Llama-3.1, DeepSeek-V3, Mixtral, Mamba-2, and AlphaFold2, and translate 33 operators from KernelBench that are validated for correctness and set with new problem sizes that achieve high TPU v6e MXU utilization. Eight of the 17 production operators ship with hand-optimized Pallas kernels from the public Tokamax library and block-size tuned to establish an expert upper-bound baseline. We evaluate four feedback-driven methods on generating candidate Pallas kernels for JAXBench. Across the full suite with Gemini 3 Flash, we find that target-specific context matters more than model scale on a sparsely-documented DSL like Pallas. Conditioning on curated TPU documentation raises per-sample correctness from 5.8% to 37.3% and solves 48 of 50 benchmarks at a 1.28x geomean speedup. Search structure yields significant gains once correctness is achieved, with Autocomp's beam-search pipeline reaching a 1.36x geomean speedup over XLA. On the 8 hand-tuned kernels, Autocomp reaches 1.60x geomean over XLA, recovering most of the 2.08x Tokamax upper bound but trailing on the specialized paged and ragged attention operators. High-quality TPU kernel optimization remains a challenging task, and we release the JAXBench benchmark, evaluation harness, and baseline results to support open source contributions.

</details>


### [166] [DC-Leap: Training-Free Acceleration of dLLMs via Draft-Guided Contiguous Leaping Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20467)
*Yanhua Jiao,Tianyi Wu,Xiaoxi Sun,Yulin Li,HuiLing Zhen,Libo Qin,Baotian Hu,Zhuotao Tian,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DC-Leap是一个无需训练的框架，通过动态连续验证策略解决扩散大语言模型中联合概率依赖错误问题，在中等置信度区域实现可靠加速，最高可达105.02倍速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散大语言模型的并行解码策略因过于保守的置信度阈值（由联合概率依赖错误导致）而产生冗余去噪迭代，推理速度次优，亟需在不牺牲生成质量的前提下实现可靠加速。

Method: 提出DC-Leap框架，包含两大核心：1) 动态连续验证策略，将严格有序的因果约束融入并行解码，逐步验证token依赖关系以消除JPDE；2) 草稿引导解码机制，通过草稿跳跃多token扩展上下文，在推理时保留双向注意力的结构优势。

Result: 在标准基准测试中，DC-Leap在MBPP长序列生成上实现最高53.19倍加速，结合KV-Cache达到105.02倍加速，且生成质量与原始模型相当。

Conclusion: DC-Leap有效解决了JPDE问题，在中等置信度区域实现了扩散大语言模型的可靠且显著的加速，为高效并行解码提供了新思路。

Abstract: While parallel decoding is central to the efficiency of Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs), current strategies are often hindered by overly conservative confidence thresholds. These thresholds, necessitated by the Joint Probability Dependence Error (JPDE), result in redundant denoising iterations and suboptimal inference speeds. To overcome this, we propose DC-Leap, a training-free framework that enables reliable acceleration of dLLMs in the moderate-confidence regime. DC-Leap introduces a Dynamic Contiguous Verification strategy that integrates strictly-ordered causal constraints into the parallel decoding process. By progressively validating token dependencies, this mechanism effectively neutralizes the JPDE, enabling reliable acceleration with comparable performance. Furthermore, DC-Leap incorporates the draft-guided decoding mechanism, where the draft helps extend the context by leaping forward across multiple tokens, providing look-ahead context and retaining the structural benefits of bidirectional attention during inference. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that DC-Leap achieves substantial speedups, up to 53.19x on MBPP for long-sequence generation, and up to 105.02x when combined with KV-Cache with comparable generation quality. Code is available at https://github.com/ffh-wyls/DC-Leap .

</details>


### [167] [InferenceBench: A Benchmark for Open-Ended LLM Inference Optimization by AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20468)
*Jehyeok Yeon,Ben Rank,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出InferenceBench基准，测试AI代理在开放场景下优化LLM推理速度的能力。15种前沿代理配置虽能提升性能（最高8.08倍），但远低于简单超参数搜索（11.53倍），且代理往往集中在单一框架上，缺乏探索多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试通常在预设工作流或狭窄动作空间上评估AI代理，即使名义上开放的任务也可通过检索已知方案解决，无法区分真实优化还是记忆解。需要更开放的基准来评估代理在AI工程中的真实能力。

Method: 构建InferenceBench基准：代理需部署兼容OpenAI的推理服务器并优化LLM推理速度。每个代理获得目标LLM、一张H100 GPU、优化场景和2小时时间预算。包含四个优化场景（预填充延迟、解码延迟、并发请求吞吐量、三者平衡），评估15种前沿代理配置。

Result: 代理可靠地提升了性能（最高8.08倍），通常匹配或超过默认设置的vLLM引擎（4.05倍），但低于相同时间预算下的简单超参数搜索（11.53倍）。代理列举了许多相关优化技术，但绝大多数集中在一个推理框架上，只测试少量配置，剩余预算用于重新测量、修复或优化超参数而非探索不同策略。

Conclusion: 瓶颈不在于领域知识，而在于提出多样化配置、系统评估并提交最佳方案的能力。InferenceBench反映了代理在开放式AI工程环境中的能力，记忆解只能带来有限改进。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used to automate research and development tasks, yet existing benchmarks typically evaluate them on prescribed workflows or narrow action spaces. Even nominally open-ended tasks can often be solved by retrieving a well-known recipe and tuning a few hyperparameters, making it unclear whether strong results reflect genuine optimization or memorized solutions. We introduce InferenceBench, where an agent must deploy an OpenAI-compatible inference server and optimize the speed of LLM inference. Each agent receives a target LLM, one H100 GPU, an optimization scenario, and a wall-clock time budget of two hours. Three optimization scenarios isolate distinct bottlenecks of inference (prefill latency, decode latency, and concurrent request throughput) and a fourth balances all three at the same time. Across 15 frontier agent configurations, agents reliably improve over a naive PyTorch baseline (up to $8.08\times$) and often match or exceed serving engines with default settings ($4.05\times$ for vLLM), but still fall below a simple hyperparameter search under the same time budget (up to $11.53\times$). Qualitative analysis of agent trajectories shows that although agents enumerate many relevant optimization techniques, they overwhelmingly converge on a single inference framework. They test only a few distinct configurations and spend the remaining budget re-measuring, repairing, or optimizing hyperparameters rather than exploring substantially different strategies. This suggests the bottleneck is not domain knowledge, but the ability to propose diverse configurations, evaluate them systematically, and submit the best identified solution. Overall, InferenceBench reflects the ability of agents to operate in an open-ended AI engineering setting, where memorized solutions lead to limited improvements.

</details>


### [168] [DecodeShare: Tracing the Shared Subspace of LLM Decode-Time Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20469)
*Zishan Shao,Lixun Zhang,Kangning Cui,Yixiao Wang,Ting Jiang,Hancheng Ye,Qinsi Wang,Zhixu Du,Yuzhe Fu,Fan Yang,Danyang Zhuo,Yiran Chen,Hai Helen Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DecodeShare协议，发现LLM在解码阶段存在一个跨任务共享的低维子空间，移除该子空间会显著降低决策性能，并验证了其在激活引导中的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM在预填充阶段的任务通用结构，但解码阶段的隐藏状态是否也存在任务通用的结构尚不明确，作者希望探究解码阶段是否存在跨任务共享的子空间及其因果作用。

Method: 提出DecodeShare协议，识别解码阶段隐藏状态中跨任务共享的低维子空间，并通过在解码时移除该子空间来检验其因果作用，同时与预填充阶段导出的子空间和随机子空间进行比较。

Result: 在相同干预预算下，干扰解码共享子空间对决策性能的损害远大于干扰预填充子空间或随机子空间；该子空间与激活引导中的常见引导方向重叠，投影去除该子空间可分离功能角色。

Conclusion: 解码阶段的共享子空间虽紧凑但可作为高杠杆因果通道，在解码时评估引导向量比预填充代理信号更可靠，对下游部署有实际意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) handle many tasks with one set of parameters, but under KV-cached inference it is unclear what task-general structure, if any, is used at decode time rather than during prefill. We propose DecodeShare, a protocol that identifies a low-dimensional subspace consistently shared across tasks in decode-time hidden states, and then tests its causal role by removing that subspace only during decoding. In our experiments, disturbing the discovered shared subspace degrades decision performance far more than disturbing either a prefill-derived or random subspace under the same intervention budget. We further show this decode-shared subspace has practical consequences for activation steering: common steering directions can overlap the task-general decode channel. Projecting out this shared subspace directly separates the functional roles of the two components, while evaluating steering vectors at decode-time yields more reliable signal for downstream deployment than prefill-based proxies. Despite its compactness, the shared subspace can serve as a high-leverage causal channel at decode time. Code is available at: https://github.com/Zishan-Shao/decodeshare.git.

</details>


### [169] [PlanE: Meta Planning of Data, Tuning, and Inference for Extractive-based LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20470)
*Jiacheng Wang,Weiyan Zhang,Guangya Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PlanE是一个面向抽取式LLM的规划框架，包含数据分解、指令微调和提示推理，并引入DTI规划器以选择最优基座模型与数据-微调-推理组合，提升特定任务构建效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调数据集规模庞大导致标注成本高，且缺乏针对特定任务优化LLM的方法。

Method: 提出PlanE框架，包含数据分解、指令微调和提示推理三个环节；并引入DTI规划器，用于选择最优基座LLM及其数据-微调-推理组合。

Result: 实验从两个视角验证了PlanE的有效性：(1) 相同基座模型在不同数据集上；(2) 相同数据集在不同基座模型上。同时验证了DTI规划器在不同优化目标下的泛化能力。

Conclusion: PlanE能有效提升LLM在特定任务上的构建效率，DTI规划器具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Enhancing the task-specific capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) primarily requires substantial instruction-tuning datasets. However, the sheer volume of such data imposes a considerable annotation cost, and a lack of optimization methods for tailoring LLMs to specific tasks. To address the above issues, we propose a \textbf{Plan}ning framework for constructing \textbf{E}xtractive-based LLMs called \textbf{PlanE}, which includes data decomposition, instruction tuning, and prompt inference. Additionally, we introduce a Data-Tuning-Inference (DTI) planner, aimed at selecting the optimal base-LLM and its DTI combinations for specific datasets to improve construction efficiency. The experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our PlanE from two views: (1) across different datasets using the same base-LLM, and (2) on the same dataset using different base-LLMs. Furthermore, we validate the generalizability of the proposed DTI planner under different optimization objectives. The codes are publicly available at https://github.com/gugugu-469/PlanE.

</details>


### [170] [Benchmarking the Personalization Capabilities of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20471)
*Ashutosh Srivastava,Siddharth Yedlapati,Vinay Aggarwal,Yaman Kumar Singla,Shashwat Dixit,Jitendra Ajmera,Balaji Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SDR-Bench基准，用于评估大语言模型在经典两方个性化（贝叶斯说服框架）中的表现。实验发现，即使是最先进的模型在说服第三方（销售场景）时也存在个性化瓶颈，无法显著区分成功与不成功的沟通结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM个性化基准仅衡量发送方适配（模型服务同一用户），而经典心理学/营销中的个性化是两方问题：发送方生成消息诱导独立第三方采取行动。目前缺乏可重复的、大规模的两方个性化评估方法。

Method: 将Kamenica和Gentzkow (2011)的贝叶斯说服框架适配到生成式智能体，在销售场景中实例化。构建SDR-Bench语料库（6,279条客户成功故事，覆盖22个行业、约200家企业），采用时间约束模拟防止数据泄露。通过Fortune 100科技公司队列和12名专业销售代表进行实地部署验证。

Result: 前沿LLM和深度研究代理均出现一致的个性化瓶颈，在Fortune 100科技队列中，没有任何模型能统计上区分成功与不成功的沟通。实地部署中，48%的模型生成内容被立即评为有用，高级专家一致性达Pearson 0.82。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在两方个性化任务上存在根本性局限，SDR-Bench和SDR-Arena为可复现的大规模生成式个性化研究提供了公开基准。

Abstract: Personalization, the act of varying a message to induce action from a specific receiver while keeping sender, channel, and time fixed, has a long tradition in psychology and marketing as a two-party problem in which sender and receiver have independent objectives. Large language models remove the bounded-inventory constraint of classical retrieval-and-ranking approaches by generating a continuum of message variants conditioned on inferred receiver state, raising the question of how well current models perform personalization in the classical sense. Existing LLM personalization benchmarks measure sender-side adaptation, in which the receiver is the same user the model is serving. The two-party question, whether a generated message induces its intended action in a third party, has been investigated only through A/B tests and small-scale human studies that cannot be re-run against a new model on demand. We adapt the Bayesian Persuasion framework of Kamenica and Gentzkow (2011) to generative agents and instantiate the formulation in sales, where receiver actions are routinely logged against the outreach that induced them. We release SDR-Bench, a public corpus of 6,279 customer success stories spanning 22 industries and approximately 200 enterprises, served through a temporally constrained simulation that prevents future-data leakage. Across frontier LLMs and deep-research agents, we observe a consistent personalization plateau and on a Fortune 100 tech cohort no model statistically separates successful from unsuccessful outreach. A field deployment with 12 professional sales representatives validates the framework, with 48 percent of model-generated content rated immediately useful and senior-expert agreement at Pearson 0.82. We release SDR-Arena and SDR-Bench publicly to support reproducible study of generative personalization at scale.

</details>


### [171] [VeriSimpl: Robust Optimization Modeling from Natural Language using Simplification-based Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20474)
*Sumaya Abdul Rahman,Seckhen Ariel Andrade Cuellar,Ghani Raissov,Mohammad Raza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VeriSimpl框架，利用基于简化的验证方法，通过优化求解器生成简化诊断查询，帮助LLM验证优化建模公式的正确性，在多个基准测试中持续优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM将自然语言转化为优化公式的方法虽能生成可执行代码，但无法保证公式正确实现预期任务，缺乏有效的正确性验证机制。

Method: 提出VeriSimpl框架，核心是简化验证策略：利用优化求解器生成简化后的诊断查询，使LLM在固定全局上下文中对局部约束和变量进行推理，从而判断公式是否正确。

Result: 在多个优化基准测试上，VeriSimpl相比现有方法在准确性上取得持续改进，同时提供了一种新颖的高精度自我验证信号。

Conclusion: 简化验证策略能够有效提升LLM在自然语言到优化建模任务中的正确性和可靠性，为可验证的自动化优化建模提供了新方向。

Abstract: Natural language interfaces can greatly benefit the accessibility and usability of optimization modeling, and recent advances in large language models (LLMs) show promise in automatically translating textual problem descriptions into executable solver formulations. However, a key challenge for existing approaches is to ensure that the inferred formulation correctly implements the intended task, even if it may execute without errors. We introduce VeriSimpl, a solver LLM framework for robust natural-language-to-optimization formalization. Our approach is based on the idea of simplification-based verification, where the optimization solver is leveraged to generate simplified diagnostic queries about a candidate formulation to allow the LLM to tractably reason about the correctness of the formulation with respect to the task description. We present such simplification strategies along different dimensions with respect to problem constraints and decision variables, which allow the LLM to reason locally under fixed global contexts. Evaluations on a range of optimization benchmarks show how our approach provides consistent improvements in accuracy over existing methods, while also providing a novel high-precision self-verification signal.

</details>


### [172] [SonicSampler: Unified Tile-Aware Kernels for LLM Sampling and Speculative Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20475)
*Pragaash Ponnusamy,Shivam Sahni,Jue Wang,Tri Dao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SonicSampler是一个统一的Triton内核套件，通过垂直融合完整的采样流水线（包括logit处理、令牌选择和投机验证）到固定的、工作负载感知的执行模型中，支持动态按请求采样行为，在批量内核中实现CUDA图兼容，并提出分层两阶段top-k算法，相比基线最高实现10倍加速，在异构投机解码场景中最高实现16倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM推理采样实现要么只加速流水线的子集，要么依赖多个内核启动，或者假设批次内采样行为同质化，限制了动态服务负载的支持并阻碍了高效的CUDA Graph执行。

Method: 提出SonicSampler，一套统一的tile感知Triton内核，将完整的采样流水线（logit处理、令牌选择、投机验证）垂直融合为固定、工作负载感知的执行模型；核心创新是一种分层两阶段top-k算法，利用LLM输出的低熵结构实现高效的大词汇表选择。

Result: 分层两阶段top-k算法相比竞争基线实现最高10倍加速；在异构投机解码工作负载中，相比最先进基线实现最高16倍加速。

Conclusion: SonicSampler通过垂直融合采样流水线、支持动态按请求采样行为、保持CUDA图兼容性，在批次执行中兼顾灵活性与高性能，显著加速了LLM推理的采样阶段。

Abstract: Sampling in LLM inference comprises a combinatorial set of logit processing, token selection, and verification operations for speculative decoding. However, existing implementations either accelerate only subsets of this pipeline, rely on multiple kernel launches, or assume homogeneous sampling behavior across a batch, limiting support for dynamic serving workloads and preventing efficient CUDA Graph execution. We present $\textbf{SonicSampler}$, a unified suite of tile-aware Triton kernels that vertically fuses the complete sampling pipeline into a fixed, workload-aware execution model. Our kernels support dynamic per-request sampling behaviors, including grammar-constrained decoding, repetition, frequency and presence penalties, logit bias, temperature scaling, top-$k$ / top-$p$ / min-$p$ filtering, and speculative verification - within a single batched kernel while remaining fully CUDA Graph-compatible. Central to our approach is a novel hierarchical two-stage top-$k$ algorithm that achieves up to $\textbf{10x speedup}$ over competitive baselines and exploits the low-entropy structure of LLM outputs to enable efficient selection over large vocabularies. Across heterogeneous speculative decoding workloads, SonicSampler achieves up to $\textbf{16x speedup}$ over state-of-the-art baselines while preserving flexible batched execution.

</details>


### [173] [Benchmarking Large Language Models on Multi-Sensor Physical Hazard Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20476)
*Faizan Iqbal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 测试五个大语言模型（ChatGPT-4o、Gemini 2.5 Flash、DeepSeek、Kimi、Llama 3.1 8B）对多传感器物理危险数据的评估能力，发现所有模型在多传感器联合超标但未达单个阈值时均无法生成预警信号，而单传感器阈值违规检测近乎完美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估现有大语言模型在多传感器物理安全监测场景中的实际表现，特别是多传感器联合风险评估这一关键但被忽视的安全缺口。

Method: 设计60个场景（多传感器联合评估、响应比例性、模式区分三类），以温度0.0进行1800次API调用测试，比较结构化表格与纯文本格式的效果。

Result: 所有模型在多传感器场景（A类）得分接近零（Q2: 0.000-0.208；Q3: 0.000-0.592），单传感器场景（B类）准确率极高（Q1: 0.975-1.000）。结构化表格无一致优势，ChatGPT-4o在纯文本下表现显著更好（p=0.001）。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型存在系统性的多传感器联合风险盲区，在物理安全监测系统中直接部署存在重大安全隐患。

Abstract: We present an empirical benchmark evaluating how five large language models assess multisensor physical hazard data. Testing 60 scenarios across three categories - multi-sensor joint assessment, response proportionality, and pattern disambiguation - with 1,800 API calls at temperature 0.0, we find that all tested models consistently produced no precautionary warning signal across the tested scenarios where multiple sensors are simultaneously elevated below their individual safety limits, while achieving near-perfect accuracy on single-sensor threshold violations. All five models (ChatGPT-4o, Gemini 2.5 Flash, DeepSeek, Kimi, Llama 3.1 8B) score near zero on Category A multi-sensor scenarios (Q2: 0.000-0.208; Q3: 0.000-0.592) compared to strong performance on single-sensor scenarios (Category B Q1: 0.975-1.000). Structured tabular formatting shows no consistent advantage over plain prose; ChatGPT-4o performs significantly better under prose (p = 0.001). These findings have direct implications for practitioners deploying the tested models in physical safety monitoring systems.

</details>


### [174] [Semi-Supervised Text-Attributed Graph Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20477)
*Yurui Lai,Samir Moustafa,Renchi Yang,Tsz Nam Chan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于Wasserstein距离的统一半监督框架WSD，通过图-文本协同编码、图草图绘制和LLM文本合成模块，解决文本属性图在大语言模型下的可扩展性问题，在GNN和LLM下游任务中实现了最优的性能-压缩权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本属性图（TAG）表示学习方法在大语言模型（LLM）下面临严重的可扩展性瓶颈；现有数据蒸馏方法未能捕捉图与文本模态间的复杂交互，在半监督场景下难以应对标签稀缺问题，且无法生成下游LLM任务所需的人类可读文本属性。

Method: 提出WSD框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 图-文本协同编码模块，采用双路径编码器（图感知+图无关）在协同自训练方案中获取可靠伪标签并融合互补特征；(2) 基于Wasserstein距离的图草图绘制算法；(3) 基于聚类的关键词提取驱动的LLM文本合成模块，为压缩节点生成连贯、可读的摘要。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，WSD在GNN和LLM下游任务中均实现了最优的性能-压缩权衡，支持有效且高效的TAG学习与分析。

Conclusion: WSD框架通过统一半监督学习、Wasserstein距离引导的图压缩和LLM文本合成，有效解决了TAG在大语言模型场景下的可扩展性、标签稀缺性和可读性三大挑战。

Abstract: {\em Text-Attributed Graphs} (TAGs) have emerged as an expressive data model for integrating graph topology with rich textual semantics. Existing representation learning methods over TAGs suffer from severe scalability bottlenecks, particularly together with {\em Large Language Models} (LLMs). While data distillation offers a promising data-centric solution, existing methods fail to capture the complex interplay between graph and text modalities, struggle with the label scarcity inherent in semi-supervised settings, and lack the ability to produce the human-readable textual attributes required for downstream LLM-based tasks.
  To address these challenges, we propose \algo{}, a unified semi-supervised framework guided by the {\em Wasserstein Distance} (WSD). Grounded in our empirical findings on real TAGs, \algo{} introduces a graph-text collaborative encoding module that utilizes dual-pathway encoders (graph-aware and -free) within a collaborative self-training scheme to harvest reliable pseudo-labels and fuse complementary graph-text features. Furthermore, we develop a theoretically grounded WSD-based graph sketching algorithm and a cost-effective LLM text synthesis module, which leverages cluster-based keyword extraction to generate coherent, human-readable summaries for condensed nodes. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that \algo{} achieves a state-of-the-art performance-compression trade-off in terms of both GNN- and LLM-based downstream tasks, enabling effective and efficient TAG learning or analytics.

</details>


### [175] [Beyond Liars' Bench: The Impact of Lie Typology, Depth, and Sparsity on Deception Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20479)
*Amr Moustafa,Max Feser,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了影响大语言模型欺骗行为检测的多个因素（表征深度、探针表达力、稀疏特征、谎言类型），发现训练数据的谎言类型对检测性能影响最大，欺骗检测是一个高度依赖表征的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探针在跨域检测中表现不佳，训练于一种谎言类型难以泛化到其他类型的欺骗场景，因此需要系统研究影响检测性能的关键因素。

Method: 在标准基准训练数据基础上，补充了包含捏造、遗漏、夸大等多种欺骗类型的多样化数据集，并比较了七种探针类型，系统分析了表征深度、探针表达力、稀疏自编码器特征以及谎言类型学对检测效果的影响。

Result: 最优表征深度高度依赖数据集；表达力更强的探针相比线性基线仅有选择性提升；稀疏自编码器特征与密集隐层状态表现相似；训练数据和谎言类型的选择显著改变检测效果。

Conclusion: 欺骗检测是一个高度依赖表征的问题，尤其训练数据的谎言类型学对检测性能影响最大。

Abstract: Training probes to detect deceptive outputs from large language models is still an open problem. Recent work has demonstrated that detection probes fail especially in out-of-domain scenarios -- training on one type of lie does not transfer well to deception scenarios involving other types of lies. In this work, we conduct a systematic study on how various factors impact detection performance: representation depth, probe expressivity, sparse feature representations, and the lie typology of the training data. To this end, we augment standard benchmark training data with a supplementary dataset containing diverse types of deception, including fabrication, omission, and exaggeration examples. Analyzing these factors across seven probe types, our experimental results show that the optimal representation depth is highly dataset-dependent, more expressive probes provide only selective gains over linear baselines, and sparse autoencoder features perform similarly to dense hidden states. Ultimately, we demonstrate that the choice of training data and lie typology substantially changes detectability, highlighting that deception detection is a highly representation-dependent problem.

</details>


### [176] [Enabling Scalable Topology Inference in Distribution Systems via Constrained Multi-Source Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20480)
*Haoran Li,Lihao Mai,Muhao Guo,Jiaqi Wu,Yang Weng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于约束推理的配电网拓扑识别方法，利用异构证据（电气测量、空间记录和运行约束）改进公用事业公司提供的基础拓扑，在包含8000多个智能电表的三个馈线上实现了超过95%的拓扑重建精度，同时显著降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际配电网中，由于数据异构且不完善，维护可靠的拓扑连接记录十分困难。现有方法仅依赖电气相似性或空间记录，在密集馈线和不一致的元数据条件下不可靠。

Method: 将拓扑识别建模为约束推理问题：在公用事业基础拓扑基础上，检测不一致分配，在约束邻域内进行局部重连以保障可扩展性，迭代强制执行物理可行性约束。同时提出基于伪造驱动的可靠性指标，评估每个推断连接相对于替代可行分配的置信度。

Result: 在美国大型公用事业公司的三个馈线（含8000多个AMI电表）上验证，拓扑重建准确率超过95%，计算效率显著优于全局推理方法。纯相关性方法在密集城市馈线中会产生模糊分配，而结合电气测量与空间及运行约束可实现鲁棒恢复。

Conclusion: 结合电气测量、空间约束和运行约束的约束推理框架，能够在实际部署条件下实现鲁棒且可扩展的配电网拓扑恢复，并为公用事业公司提供可靠性评估指标以优先安排验证工作。

Abstract: Accurate distribution system topology is essential for outage localization, voltage analytics, and operation of distribution grids, yet maintaining reliable connectivity records remains challenging in practice due to heterogeneous and imperfect utility data. Existing topology identification methods often rely primarily on electrical similarity or spatial records alone, which become unreliable in dense feeders and under inconsistent metadata conditions. This paper formulates distribution topology identification as a constrained inference problem that refines a utility-provided base topology using heterogeneous evidence while enforcing spatial feasibility and physical operational constraints. Instead of reconstructing connectivity from scratch, the proposed framework detects inconsistent assignments, performs localized reconnection within constrained neighborhoods to ensure scalability, and iteratively enforces physical feasibility to produce operationally consistent topology estimates. In addition, a falsification-driven reliability metric evaluates how strongly each inferred connection is supported relative to alternative feasible assignments, enabling utilities to prioritize verification efforts while preserving system-wide observability. The framework is validated using operational data from three feeders comprising more than $8{,}000$ AMI meters in collaboration with a large U.S. utility. Results demonstrate over $95\%$ topology reconstruction accuracy while significantly reducing computational effort compared with global inference approaches. The study further shows that correlation-based methods alone produce ambiguous assignments in dense urban feeders, whereas combining electrical measurements with spatial and operational constraints enables robust and scalable topology recovery under realistic deployment conditions.

</details>


### [177] [Routing Without Training: Controllable-Ratio LLM Offloading via Reliability Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20481)
*Evan Chen,Shiqiang Wang,Kevin S Chan,Su Wang,Christopher Brinton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CARGO框架，利用本地模型自身的推理时响应一致性决定是否将任务卸载到云端，无需训练路由器，在多种任务和模型上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有本地-云端协作方法依赖训练路由器或协作感知微调，将路由行为绑定到特定运行模式，缺乏灵活性和适应性。

Method: 提出CARGO框架：通过提示变体采样估计本地模型响应一致性，采用贝叶斯早期停止实现样本高效的不确定性控制，并通过轻量级部署时校准支持任意目标协作比例。

Result: 在多种推理和问答任务、多个本地LLM家族和规模、预训练和微调模型上，CARGO持续优于其他免训练基线，并在多个设置中超越有监督学习的路由器。

Conclusion: 有效的本地-云端协作可以直接从本地模型的内在响应行为中涌现，无需额外的训练路由器。

Abstract: Local-cloud collaboration is a practical way to deploy large language models under resource constraints, but existing methods often rely on trained routers or collaboration-aware finetuning that tie routing behavior to a particular operating regime. In this work, we show that such training may be unnecessary: the local model's own inference-time agreement across sampled responses already provides a strong signal for deciding when to trust local execution and when to offload to a stronger cloud model. We propose CARGO, a training-free routing framework that estimates this agreement through prompt-varied sampling, applies Bayesian early stopping for sample-efficient uncertainty control, and supports arbitrary target collaboration ratios through lightweight deployment-time calibration. Across diverse reasoning and question-answering tasks, multiple local LLM families and scales, and both pretrained and finetuned local models, CARGO consistently outperforms other training-free baselines and in several settings surpasses supervised learned routers. These results suggest that effective and adaptable local-cloud collaboration can emerge directly from the local model's intrinsic response behavior, without requiring an additional trained router.

</details>


### [178] [PersonaTrail: Benchmarking Personalized Web Agents through Browsing Trails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20482)
*Seungbin Yang,Chaewoon Ki,Dohyun Lee,Jaegul Choo,ChaeHun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PersonaTrail基准测试和PACMem框架，用于评估和提升网络智能体根据用户浏览历史进行个性化任务执行的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试无法捕捉网络智能体从用户浏览历史中推断缺失上下文进行个性化的能力，大多局限于完全显式的指令或简化历史。

Method: 提出PersonaTrail基准测试，利用真实浏览轨迹评估智能体推断用户偏好和回忆历史信息的能力；同时提出PACMem框架，将原始浏览历史分解为事实性记忆（总结单次会话）和偏好性记忆（提炼重复行为模式），推理时检索最相关条目指导个性化导航。

Result: PACMem在两类任务上均持续优于现有基于记忆的基线方法。

Conclusion: PersonaTrail填补了用户个性化网络智能体评估的空缺，PACMem通过结构化的记忆分解和检索有效提升了智能体的个性化表现。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have enabled web agents to autonomously execute complex tasks. In practice, users frequently provide underspecified instructions, requiring agents to infer the missing context from their raw browsing histories. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this form of personalization, as they either restrict tasks to fully explicit prompts or abstract web interaction history into simplified forms. To bridge this gap, we introduce PersonaTrail, a benchmark for personalized web agents operating in a managed open web environment. By leveraging realistic browsing trajectories as user history, PersonaTrail evaluates an agent's ability to infer user preferences and recall information from past browsing sessions. We further propose Preference-Aware Contextual Memory (PACMem), a framework that decomposes raw browsing histories into two types of structured memory: factual memories that summarize individual sessions and preference memories that distill recurring behavioral patterns. At inference time, the agent retrieves the most relevant entries from these memories to guide personalized navigation. Extensive experiments show that PACMem consistently outperforms existing memory-based baselines on both tasks.

</details>


### [179] [Tractable Hierarchical Control of Autoregressive Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20483)
*Max Scribner,Antonio Vergari,Vaishak Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多项式时间算法，用于确保自回归大语言模型生成符合LR(k)上下文无关语法的有效输出，比现有指数时间方法有显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大语言模型集成到形式系统中时，确保其生成语法有效的输出（如SQL、JSON等）是必要前提，但现有方法处理LR(k)语法需要指数时间，效率低下。

Method: 通过将LLM蒸馏为可处理的概率模型，利用LR(k)语法约束进行引导和掩码，实现对任意有限持续时间的LR(k)语法满足性在多项式时间内计算。

Result: 证明了任意LR(k)语法的满足性可在多项式时间内计算，而此前方法需要指数时间。

Conclusion: 该结果使得对LLM生成进行高效约束和引导成为可能，能够生成更符合形式语法约束的高质量输出。

Abstract: Constraining the generation of autoregressive large language models (LLMs) is an important component of integrating language models into formal systems. In the generation of code and data for tasks like program synthesis, ensuring that language models produce syntactically valid output is a prerequisite for processing such output. These languages (such as SQL or JSON) are often designed as $LR(k)$ context-free grammars. By distilling the LLM to a tractable probabilistic model, its autoregressive generation can be steered and masked to incorporate the probability of satisfying logical constraints, ensuring high quality output that is guaranteed to be valid. This paper demonstrates that the satisfaction of any $LR(k)$ grammar of finite duration can be calculated in polynomial time, an improvement over the exponential time of applying previous methods to such grammars. This result enables efficient constraint and steering of LLM generation towards output that better satisfies formal syntactic constraints.

</details>


### [180] [The Devil is in the Spectrum: Mitigating Representation Collapse in LLMs via Topologically Regularized Side-Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20484)
*Yiheng Tao,Kaiwen Cheng,Yao Lu,Chang Liu,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出拓扑正则化侧路径（TRSP）机制，通过三角盒结构和长度感知门控，解决LLM长上下文中的表征坍塌问题（同质化坍塌与孤立坍塌），在8倍训练长度下保持83%准确率，远超现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在长上下文中存在表征坍塌问题，表现为两种极端：注意力沉导致的同质化坍塌（秩不足）和局部注意力导致的孤立坍塌（上下文断裂）。标准机制难以平衡混合效率与信息容量之间的内在权衡。

Method: 提出Topologically Regularized Side-Path (TRSP)，一种非侵入式架构干预。包含两个关键组件：参数无关的三角盒结构（Triangular Box）用于正则化token交互拓扑，轻量级长度感知门控（length-aware gate）进行缩放。通过近端耦合保持有效秩，远端传播支持非退化混合，实现谱平衡。

Result: 在通用能力和长上下文基准上均取得显著提升。在NoLiMa基准上，训练长度8倍外推时保持83%准确率，分别超越Differential Transformer和Gated Attention约30和50个百分点。

Conclusion: TRSP通过拓扑正则化在注意力动态中实现谱平衡，有效解决了长上下文表征坍塌问题，且不改变核心注意力机制，是一种轻量高效的架构改进方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are fundamentally limited by representation collapse, a bottleneck that severely degrades long-context performance. We identify that existing approaches risk drifting into one of two pathological extremes: homogenization collapse (e.g., attention sinks causing rank deficiency) and isolation collapse (e.g., local attention causing context disconnection). Through spectral analysis of attention dynamics, we derive an intrinsic trade-off between mixing efficiency (spectral gap) and information capacity (effective rank) that standard mechanisms struggle to balance. To resolve this dilemma, we propose the Topologically Regularized Side-Path (TRSP), a non-invasive architectural intervention that achieves spectral balance. TRSP employs a parameter-free Triangular Box mechanism, scaled by a lightweight, length-aware gate, to regularize the token interaction topology. By integrating proximal coupling to preserve effective rank and distal propagation to support non-degenerate mixing, TRSP promotes a geometrically healthier transition operator without altering core attention. Experiments show significant improvements across general capabilities and long-context benchmarks. Notably, on NoLiMa at $8\times$ the training length, TRSP retains $83\%$ accuracy and surpasses the Differential Transformer and Gated Attention by approximately 30 and 50 percentage points, respectively. Code available at: https://github.com/Eziotao-tyd/TRSP.

</details>


### [181] [Expectation Alignment of Language Models for Real-World User Expectations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20485)
*Miaomiao Li,Yang Wang,Bin Liang,Shudong Liu,Zhiwei Zhang,Kam-Fai Wong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文首次系统研究了真实用户期望与大语言模型之间的对齐问题，发现现有模型虽在标准基准上表现优异，但难以满足用户的真实期望。为此，作者构建了基于真实用户期望的基准ExpectBench，并提出了轻量级框架LENS，通过显式建模用户潜在期望来生成更符合用户需求的回复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法（如模型启发式、专家评分、用户模拟）无法捕捉真实用户期望的多样性和细微差别，导致模型看似表现良好实则与用户实际需求错位，亟需系统研究用户期望与模型对齐的问题。

Method: 1) 提出了一套从真实用户交互中提取语义丰富期望的原则性流程；2) 构建了基于真实用户期望的基准ExpectBench；3) 提出了LENS（轻量级潜在期望感知回复生成框架），通过显式建模用户潜在期望来改善模型回复。

Result: 当前LLMs难以满足和预判用户期望，揭示了根本性的对齐错位问题；LENS框架能持续提升期望满意度，强调了显式建模用户期望对实现真实人类- AI对齐的重要性。

Conclusion: 真实用户期望与模型能力之间存在显著鸿沟，显式建模用户期望是提升人机对齐的关键方向，LENS框架为此提供了有效的轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance on standard benchmarks, yet it remains largely unexplored whether they truly meet user expectations. Existing evaluation approaches, relying on model heuristics, expert rubrics, or user simulation, fail to capture the diversity and subtlety of real human expectations, causing models to appear competent while misaligning with what users actually seek. We present the first systematic study of user expectations in real-world LLM interactions, proposing a principled procedure to extract semantically rich expectations and introducing ExpectBench, a benchmark grounded in real user expectations. Analyses reveal that current LLMs struggle to satisfy and anticipate what users hope to obtain, highlighting a fundamental source of misalignment. Building on these observations, we propose LENS, a lightweight latent expectation-aware response generation framework. LENS enables models to internalize user expectations and generate better-aligned responses, consistently improving expectation satisfaction and underscoring the importance of explicitly modeling user expectations for realistic human-AI alignment.

</details>


### [182] [OPTScientist: Multi-Agent Discovery of Typed Optimizer Programs for Transformer Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20486)
*Zhongzheng Li,Tiancan Feng,Wenhao Li,Qingsong Ran,Shikun Feng,Xiaoyuan Zhang,Yue Wang,Xiaoguang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OPTScientist，一个基于理论指导、多智能体协作的优化器自动发现框架，通过类型化领域特定语言（DSL）和演化搜索，在受限空间中自动发现新型优化器RS-MR，在Transformer预训练任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器自动发现方法要么在无约束代码空间中搜索（灵活但易产生无效程序），要么在窄参数化家族中搜索（稳定但限制创新性），缺少一种兼具灵活性与稳定性的自动化优化器发现方法。

Method: OPTScientist包含四个角色智能体（理论家、设计者、工程师、评审者）协同工作；优化器候选通过方向、缩放、预处理、正则化、状态和分组六个模块在类型化DSL中表达；结合演化搜索与DSL扩展机制，当重复失败出现时自动提出小规模DSL扩展以突破表示瓶颈。

Result: 发现RS-MR，一种精简状态矩阵优化器，在Transformer预训练任务中优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一条基于理论、类型化程序、编译器验证和闭环实验的自动化优化器科学发现路径。

Abstract: Designing optimizers for modern deep learning remains a challenging scientific problem, requiring the joint consideration of optimization geometry, state dynamics, numerical stability, implementation constraints, and empirical generalization. Existing automated optimizer discovery methods typically search either over unconstrained code spaces or within narrowly parameterized optimizer families. The former is flexible but often produces invalid or uninterpretable programs, while the latter is stable but limits novelty. We introduce OPTScientist, a theory-guided multi-agent framework for optimizer discovery in a typed domain-specific language (DSL). OPTScientist formulates optimizer design as a constrained scientific search process, where candidate updates are expressed through direction, scaling, preconditioning, regularization, state, and grouping modules. Four role agents, Theorist, Designer, Engineer, and Reviewer, collaborate within a single orchestration loop to propose hypotheses, synthesize DSL candidates, compile and evaluate optimizers, and critique results. To overcome the limitations of a fixed search space, OPTScientist combines evolutionary search over optimizer programs with a second-stage mechanism that proposes small DSL extensions when repeated failures reveal representational bottlenecks. Using this framework, we discover RS-MR, a reduced-state matrix optimizer that improves transformer pretraining over strong baselines under our native evaluation protocol. Our results suggest a path toward automated optimizer science grounded in theory, typed programs, compiler validation, and closed-loop experimentation.

</details>


### [183] [Directional Hallucinations: Ideological Drift in News-Grounded LLM Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20487)
*Chendi Wang,Liam Cunningham,Tom Yishay,Jieying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可复现的测量框架，通过检测大型语言模型在政治新闻问答中的幻觉（hallucination）现象，发现幻觉内容存在显著的左倾意识形态漂移，且与模型生成的不确定性相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地被用于回答政治信息问题，尤其是在选举相关场景中，事实错误和意识形态扭曲的风险很高。需要一种可复现的方法来诊断和量化这种意识形态漂移。

Method: 使用21,727篇来自QBias的美国政治新闻文章，步骤包括：(i)生成文章特定问题，(ii)从三个开源模型和一个闭源模型获取基于文档的答案，(iii)通过参考对比检测句子级幻觉，(iv)用微调立场分类器对幻觉句子进行意识形态倾向分类，(v)分析输出logits以关联token级不确定性与幻觉和漂移。

Result: 不同模型的幻觉率差异显著，集中在争议性话题；来源意识形态差异对幻觉频率影响较小；但幻觉内容存在稳健的左倾漂移——大多数幻觉句子被分类为左倾，包括来自右倾来源的幻觉；logit分析显示幻觉出现在高熵生成上下文中，某些模型中不确定性也预测左倾漂移。

Conclusion: 讨论了该发现对审计AI中介政治信息以及设计选举相关部署中安全措施的启示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to answer questions about political information, including in election-adjacent information settings where factual errors and ideological distortions are high-stakes. We present a reproducible measurement framework that treats hallucinations, unsupported statements in document-grounded QA, as diagnostic signals of ideological drift. Using 21,727 expert-labeled U.S. political news articles from QBias spanning left, center, and right sources, we (i) generate an article-specific question, (ii) elicit document-grounded answers from three open-weight LLMs and one proprietary model, (iii) detect sentence-level hallucinations via reference-based comparison, (iv) classify the ideological valence of hallucinated sentences with a fine-tuned stance classifier, and (v) probe output logits to relate token-level uncertainty to hallucination and drift. Hallucination rates vary substantially across models and concentrate in contentious topics, while source-ideology differences in hallucination frequency are modest. In contrast, hallucination content exhibits robust leftward drift: a majority of hallucinated sentences are classified as left-leaning, including among hallucinations generated from right-leaning sources. Logit-level analysis shows hallucinations arise in high-entropy generation contexts, and in some models uncertainty also predicts leftward drift, consistent with an "uncertainty to guessing" mechanism. We discuss implications for auditing AI-mediated political information and for designing safeguards in election-relevant deployments.

</details>


### [184] [Autonomous Topology Mutation: Safe Runtime Restructuring for Multi-Agent LLM Systems with Capability, State, and Shadow Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20488)
*Bronislav Sidik,Chaya Levi,Nizzan Kimhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自治拓扑突变（ATM）机制，使多智能体LLM框架能在运行时动态重组团队结构，通过过载检测、安全不变性约束和影子验证，将过载智能体分解为专门子智能体，在保持外部身份的同时提升任务成功率从3.3%到61.7%，并实现零高隐私泄露事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数多智能体LLM框架在启动时固定团队拓扑结构，当单个智能体在运行时出现过载（如混合过多动作类别、累积工具错误、请求排队过多等）时，系统缺乏自我重组机制，导致性能下降。

Method: ATM结合遥测驱动的过载检测与三个安全不变性门控（能力单调性、状态路由完整性、影子验证），通过六信号瓶颈指数（队列深度、上下文抖动、工具错误率、角色熵、重试循环率、跨智能体等待时间）监测过载，在阈值被连续突破时将过载智能体分解为专门子智能体，父节点转为协调角色并保持外部身份，通过隐私感知路由控制状态转移。

Result: 在720次DeepSeek-V3驱动任务中，ATM分解器将代码任务成功率从3.3%提升至61.7%；完整系统将高隐私内存暴露事件从每任务2.0降至0.0，同时保持任务质量；运行时开销在p99延迟上仅增加不到500微秒。

Conclusion: ATM机制能有效解决多智能体系统运行时的过载问题，通过动态拓扑重组显著提升任务成功率，同时保证隐私安全和低延迟开销，为多智能体系统的自适应架构设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM frameworks typically fix their team topology at boot time. When an individual agent becomes overloaded at runtime, for example by mixing too many action categories, accumulating tool errors, or queueing behind too many calls, the system has no mechanism to restructure itself. We introduce Autonomous Topology Mutation (ATM), a runtime team-mutation mechanism for multi-agent LLM frameworks. ATM combines telemetry-driven overload detection with three safety invariants that gate each structural change: capability monotonicity, state-routing completeness, and shadow-before-live validation.
  ATM monitors a six-signal Bottleneck Index that includes queue depth, context thrash, tool-error rate, role entropy, retry-loop rate, and cross-agent wait time. When a warmup-calibrated threshold is breached for multiple consecutive ticks, ATM factorises the overloaded agent into specialised sub-agents and hot-swaps the parent into a coordinator role while preserving its external identity. State transfer is controlled by privacy-level-aware routing: each memory atom is routed only to a permitted child set, or explicitly dropped with a logged reason. No candidate topology receives live traffic until it has passed a shadow validation window.
  On 720 DeepSeek-V3-driven task runs with deterministic tool stubs across four ablation conditions and three workloads, the ATM factoriser split lifts code-task success from 3.3% to 61.7%. The full rail-and-distillation system reduces detected high-privacy memory exposure under a regex classifier from 2.0 to 0.0 events per task while preserving task quality. The runtime rails carrying ATM's invariants add less than 500 microseconds of p99 latency on the agent hot path. A small live-tool probe with real Python execution is included as an external-validity check. The implementation, benchmark harness, and traces are open-sourced.

</details>


### [185] [EvoSQL: Memory-Augmented Critic-Generator Co-Evolution for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20489)
*Jiawei Zhou,Jianwei Wang,Chenyu Zhou,Chaojian Shi,Ming Dong,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoSQL是一个协同进化框架，通过生成器与批评者之间的迭代交互、上下文候选记忆和执行信号验证，结合SDPO微调技术，显著提升了Text-to-SQL在复杂数据库查询上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂数据库查询需要超越一次性生成的推理能力，包括多步分解、基于执行的诊断和定向修正，现有方法在这方面存在不足。

Method: 提出EvoSQL协同进化框架，包含三部分：(1) 上下文候选记忆维护；(2) 执行信号与LLM批评双重验证；(3) 效用引导的记忆聚合更新。并引入SDPO微调注入执行感知监督。

Result: 在Spider和BIRD基准上，EvoSQL持续提升开源模型Maj@16基线，BIRD-Dev上提升1.37%到9.19%不等；SDPO初始化进一步提升了Spider-Test和BIRD-Dev上的表现。

Conclusion: 记忆基础的协同进化是实现更可靠、更泛化的Text-to-SQL系统的有效路径。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL has advanced rapidly with large language models, but complex database queries still require reasoning beyond one-shot generation, including multi-step decomposition, execution-based diagnosis, and targeted correction. We present EvoSQL, a co-evolution framework that formulates SQL synthesis as an iterative interaction between a generator and a critic. EvoSQL maintains a contextualized candidate memory, verifies SQL candidates with both execution signals and LLM-based critique, and updates its memory through utility-guided aggregation. To strengthen the underlying generator-critic pair, we further introduce a Self-Distillation Policy Optimization (SDPO) fine-tuning stage that injects execution-aware supervision into modern coding LLM backbones. Experiments on Spider and BIRD show that EvoSQL consistently improves open-source models over Maj@16 baselines, with particularly large gains on BIRD-Dev, ranging from +1.37% for Qwen3-4B to +9.19% for Qwen2.5-Coder-3B. SDPO initialization further improves selected backbones on Spider-Test and BIRD-Dev. These results suggest that memory-grounded co-evolution is an effective path toward more reliable and generalizable Text-to-SQL systems. Code is available at https://github.com/valleysprings/EvoSQL.

</details>


### [186] [CRAWO: Custom Resources for Adaptive Workload Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20490)
